Checks every not-done task against REALITY (was the PR merged? was it never opened? has the repo moved on without it? has nothing happened in months?), then presents a two-tier digest — a batch of obvious calls you approve in one go, plus the genuinely ambiguous ones one at a time. Use when a board has drifted, when tasks are piling up stale, or when picking a neglected workspace back up. ([shreyansqt/pwc](https://github.com/shreyansqt/pwc/tree/HEAD/skills/pwc-triage) · ★1)

- **pwm-log-likelihood-motif-scanning**: Use when a user provides a nucleotide sequence (e.g. FASTA) and needs rapid detection and annotation of core promoter elements or user-defined motifs, including on the antisense strand, without prior TSS annotation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/pwm-log-likelihood-motif-scanning))
- **pwm-one-hot-sequence-encoding**: Use when scored FASTA sequences centered on an anchor (e.g. TSS or motif) must be converted to one-hot tensors and scanned with JASPAR PWMs to produce a motif-by-position heatmap for downstream positional enrichment analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/pwm-one-hot-sequence-encoding))
- **pwm-score-verification**: Use when a PWM-based motif scanner must be validated end-to-end — e.g. reproducing ElemeNT 2023's log2(PWM/background) scoring with background P(G)=P(C)=GC/2, P(A)=P(T)=(1−GC)/2 on a test DNA sequence. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/pwm-score-verification))
- **pwm-weighted-high-affinity-sequence-annotation**: Use when preparing uPBM training files (35-mer sequences with experimental affinities, e.g. proteins/{protein}/{protein}_training.txt) for per-TF affinity regression where sequence variability must be reduced and PWM-matching high-affinity k-mers emphasized. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/pwm-weighted-high-affinity-sequence-annotation))

- **pwn-chain**: 从逆向走到可用利用 (Working Exploit) 的全链路工程化方法。 适用场景：拿到了二进制 + 漏洞点 + 目标环境，需要写出一个能稳定打通的 exploit（不是只能本地复现一下、远程一打就崩的脚本）。 覆盖三大方向：栈溢出 / 堆利用 / 内核 pwn。强调"CTF 本地通 → 真实远程稳定打通"的工程差距：libc 版本错配、堆喷射时序、SMEP/SMAP/KASLR、栈对齐、远程缓冲。 核心工具链：pwntools + GEF/pwndbg + ROPgadget/Ropper + one_gadget + libc-database + qemu-system 内核调试。 触发关键词：pwn、栈溢出、堆溢出、ROP、ret2libc、ret2csu、one_gadget、libc-database、堆利用、tcache、fastbin、unsorted bin、kernel pwn、kROP、SMEP、SMAP、KASLR、modprobe_path、pwntools、GEF、pwndbg。 ([zhaoxuya520/reverse-skill](https://github.com/zhaoxuya520/reverse-skill/tree/HEAD/skills/pwn-chain) · ★45k · 1.5k installs · 63 copies)
- **pwn-chain**: Full engineering workflow from reversing to a Working Exploit. Applicable scenario: you have a binary + a vulnerability point + a target environment, and need to write an exploit that lands reliably (not just something that works locally but crashes the moment you point it at remote). Covers three main directions: stack overflow / heap exploitation / kernel pwn. Emphasizes the engineering gap between "works locally in CTF" and "reliably lands against a real remote": libc version mismatches, heap spray timing, SMEP/SMAP/KASLR, stack alignment, remote buffering. Core toolchain: pwntools + GEF/p… ([dbx0/skills](https://github.com/dbx0/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/binary-re-pwn/exploitation/pwn-chain) · ★12)
- **pwn-chain**: 从漏洞点到可用利用的全链路工程化方法（working exploit / pwn chain）。能力：覆盖栈溢出、堆利用与内核 pwn 三大方向，把 CTF 本地能通的脚本打磨成远程稳定打通的 exploit，处理 libc 版本错配、堆喷射时序、SMEP/SMAP/KASLR、栈对齐与远程缓冲差异。适用场景：拿到二进制加漏洞点需要写 exploit、CTF 本地通但远程打不通、真实目标内存损坏漏洞要构造 RCE、Linux 内核驱动 ioctl bug 提权。Use when 用户提到 pwn、栈溢出、堆溢出、ROP、ret2libc、ret2csu、one_gadget、libc-database、堆利用、tcache、fastbin、unsorted bin、kernel pwn、kROP、SMEP、SMAP、KASLR、modprobe_path、pwntools、GEF、pwndbg，或提供 ELF、so、libc、vmlinux、ko 二进制与 core dump、远程 nc 地址。交付物：可复现的 pwntools exploit、连续多次的成功率验证记录与利用链说明。 ([lushun2014/lushibo-skills](https://github.com/lushun2014/lushibo-skills/tree/HEAD/pwn-chain) · ★5)
- **pwn-chain**: 从reverse engineering走到available利用 (Working Exploit) 的全链路工程化方法。 Use Cases：拿到了二进制 + vulnerability点 + targetenvironment，需要写出一个能稳定打通的 exploit（不是只能本地复现一下、远程一打就崩的script）。 覆盖三大方向：栈溢出 / heap利用 / kernel pwn。强调"CTF 本地通 → 真实远程稳定打通"的工程差距：libc version错配、heap喷射时序、SMEP/SMAP/KASLR、栈对齐、远程缓冲。 核心Toolchain：pwntools + GEF/pwndbg + ROPgadget/Ropper + one_gadget + libc-database + qemu-system kerneldebugging。 触发critical词：pwn、栈溢出、heap溢出、ROP、ret2libc、ret2csu、one_gadget、libc-database、heap利用、tcache、fastbin、unsorted bin、kernel pwn、kROP、SMEP、SMAP、KASLR、modprobe_path、pwntools、GEF、pwndbg。 ([xAmirHamza77/ReverseOps-Skill](https://github.com/xAmirHamza77/ReverseOps-Skill/tree/HEAD/skills/pwn-chain) · ★5)

- **pwn-ffi-soapysdr**: Drive PWN::FFI::SoapySDR from pwn_eval. ([0dayInc/pwn](https://github.com/0dayInc/pwn/tree/HEAD/etc/default_skills/pwn/ffi/soapy_sdr) · ★90)
- **pwn-ffi-stdio**: Drive PWN::FFI::Stdio from pwn_eval. ([0dayInc/pwn](https://github.com/0dayInc/pwn/tree/HEAD/etc/default_skills/pwn/ffi/stdio) · ★90)
- **pwn-ffi-volk**: Drive PWN::FFI::Volk from pwn_eval. ([0dayInc/pwn](https://github.com/0dayInc/pwn/tree/HEAD/etc/default_skills/pwn/ffi/volk) · ★90)
- **pwn-format-string-leak-write**: Use when solving CTF pwn challenges where user input reaches printf-family format strings, enabling stack/libc/PIE leaks, arbitrary writes with %n, GOT/return-address overwrites, or partial-byte/halfword writes. Do not use for logging bugs with constant format strings. ([loegaire/CTF_Journal](https://github.com/loegaire/CTF_Journal/tree/HEAD/writeups/ctf_skill_research/staging_skills/pwn-format-string-leak-write) · ★2)
- **pwn-fundamentals**: Use when you need to exploit a classic stack buffer overflow on an authorized target, such as a CTF pwn challenge or a lab binary you have permission to test. Covers binary triage with checksec, finding the crash offset with cyclic patterns, hijacking control flow to a win function, leaking addresses to defeat ASLR, building a ret2libc exploit, and using one_gadget shortcuts, all with pwntools 4.13 and GDB 15.x. Use when a binary reads attacker-controlled input into a fixed-size stack buffer, or when you are learning binary exploitation from the ground up. ([mizazhaider-ceh/AgentArsenal](https://github.com/mizazhaider-ceh/AgentArsenal/tree/HEAD/skills/binary/pwn-fundamentals))
- **pwn-glibc-tcache-poisoning**: Use when solving CTF pwn challenges with glibc heap bugs, tcache dup/poisoning, use-after-free, double free, safe-linking leaks, malloc/free hooks, or arbitrary allocation primitives. Do not use for non-glibc allocators or pure stack overflows. ([loegaire/CTF_Journal](https://github.com/loegaire/CTF_Journal/tree/HEAD/writeups/ctf_skill_research/staging_skills/pwn-glibc-tcache-poisoning) · ★2)
- **pwn-jsontext**: Drive PWN::JSONText from pwn_eval. ([0dayInc/pwn](https://github.com/0dayInc/pwn/tree/HEAD/etc/default_skills/pwn/json_text) · ★90)

- **pwn-request**: Use when hunting Pwn Request vulnerabilities where pull_request_target workflows checkout attacker-controlled PR code and execute it in a privileged context with access to repository secrets. Trigger on: "pwn request", "pull_request_target", "checkout PR head", "npm install in CI", "lifecycle scripts in CI", "preinstall script", "postinstall script", "package.json scripts CI", "npm ci ignore-scripts false", "actions/checkout ref pull request head sha", privileged workflow running PR code, "Gato-X", supply chain via PR lifecycle scripts. ([ShulkwiSEC/bb-huge](https://github.com/ShulkwiSEC/bb-huge/tree/HEAD/skills/curated/pwn-request) · ★27 · 5 copies)
- **pwn-ret2libc-rop-chain**: Use when solving CTF pwn challenges with stack overflow, NX enabled, libc leak, ROP gadgets, ret2libc, ret2plt, ret2csu, one_gadget, SROP-like stack control, or staged return chains. Do not use for heap-only bugs or shellcode-only challenges. ([loegaire/CTF_Journal](https://github.com/loegaire/CTF_Journal/tree/HEAD/writeups/ctf_skill_research/staging_skills/pwn-ret2libc-rop-chain) · ★2)
